   From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia
   (Redirected from [76](6756) 1978 VX3)

   The following is a partial [77]list of minor planets, running from
   minor-planet number 6001 through 7000, inclusive. The primary data for
   this and other partial lists is based on [78]JPL's "Small-Body Orbital
   Elements"^[79][1] and data available from the [80]Minor Planet
   Center.^[81][2]^[82][3] [83]Critical list information is also provided
   by the MPC,^[84][2]^[85][3] unless otherwise specified from [86]Lowell
   Observatory.^[87][4] A [88]detailed description of the table's columns
   and additional sources are given on the main page including a complete
   [89]list of every page in this series, and a [90]statistical break-up
   on the [91]dynamical classification of minor planets.

   Also see the summary list of all named bodies in [92]numerical and
   [93]alphabetical order, and the corresponding [94]naming citations for
   the number range of this particular list. New namings may only be added
   to this list after official publication, as the preannouncement of
   names is condemned by the [95]Working Group for Small Bodies
   Nomenclature of the [96]International Astronomical Union.
     [97]Near-Earth obj.
     [98]MBA (inner)
     [99]MBA (outer)
     [100]Centaur

     [101]Mars-crosser   [102]MBA (middle)     [103]Jupiter trojan
   [104]Trans-Neptunian obj.
     Unclassified
